# Platforma CRM native mobile — local read flows

Approved runtime: Swift/SwiftUI on iOS and Kotlin/Jetpack Compose on Android. Separate clients use the existing CRM backend; no KMP. This directory replaces the old RN client only. Backend apps/mobile, web, shared tokens/config, root CI and Git history are preserved.

Current backend-connection preparation and remaining inputs: [CONNECT-BACKEND.md](CONNECT-BACKEND.md); non-secret required inputs: [connection.public.env.example](connection.public.env.example). Runtime/session/network binding remains explicit; these files do not configure a server or run an operation.

## Current scope

Debug-only synthetic Business selection -> Appointment list/read-only daily interval agenda -> same-ID detail, plus Clients -> local search -> contact card -> return, and Inbox -> local search/unread filter -> text conversation -> return, and Tasks -> local title/status filter -> description/comments -> return. Loading, empty, denied/not-found/error, retry, capped/partial data, nullable resource and stale Business-context responses are handled. List and calendar share the same collection/IDs. No Lead/Deal wizard, booking/status/payment/AI command, offline outbox, login flow, push or Market source.

SwiftUI startup coalesces repeated starts while bootstrap is pending. A recoverable bootstrap error clears loading and allows an ordinary retry using the same fixture context.

Each detail read has a separate request identity, including retries for the same Appointment ID. A newer request, leaving detail or switching Business/date invalidates older responses and errors. A late response cannot restore an earlier readable title after a newer redacted response. These navigation/read guards are shared by both native cores; they do not change backend permissions or appointment lifecycle.
The Android ViewModel publishes the accepted detail and updated list together, so returning to the list/calendar also uses the latest permitted fields. Android system Back is registered only while a detail is loading, showing an error or open, and uses the same handler as the visible Back button. Returning preserves Business/day/list/partial state and does not reload the list; late detail results remain invalidated. Root Back stays with the OS. Actual button/gesture/reader device acceptance remains open.

`contracts/fixtures` is synthetic, shared by both clients/tests. Visible fixture mode is a system status; it is never an API-error fallback. Release compositions show API-not-configured. The original Appointment adapters use only the existing GET bootstrap/list/detail routes and checked `business/date/limit` parameters. Live transport/session composition is intentionally not configured: accepted current backend SHA/projection/redaction/branch compatibility remains pending. No live calls were made. Fixed fixture dates are injected only by explicit debug composition; ordinary view models use the current instant/business timezone. Pure native request builders validate an explicitly supplied HTTPS origin, keep paths inside the mobile API, percent-encode query values and build GET descriptors without reading credentials or opening connections. Timeout is caller-supplied; no production timeout/retry policy is invented. Read `contracts/source-evidence.json` for exact source paths and gaps.

List projection does not contain specialist information or conversation provenance. Detail resource/null fields are used only from its scoped response. No cross-entity lookup or invented dialogue link. Unknown status strings remain data, not newly approved lifecycle enums. Partial list totals are not treated as complete pagination; no guessed cursor/offset endpoint. The first slice has 21 RU/KK/EN UI keys selected by the device language (region tags normalized; unsupported locales fall back to EN). Existing CRM terminology is reused; six new technical-status translations remain drafts pending language review, recorded in `contracts/ui-copy-sources.json`. User-entered names and API enums are preserved. Device/reader/visual localization acceptance remains open.

`codex/mobile-max` branch touching native source/workflow can run native-only CI
without triggering unrelated backend jobs. No push/run has been authorized or
performed by this local configuration change. Backend/frontend jobs and triggers are preserved.

no provider credentials, no deployment or artifact upload. Android requires an existing
SDK 35/build-tools 35.0.0 and does not install SDKs or accept licenses. Java/Gradle and

no branch-protection/account settings were changed.

Live API integration still requires accepted backend/session/read projection contracts.
Language review of the six new system-status translations and device/reader/visual acceptance remain unfinished criteria.

## Offline continuation verification

The latest independent offline slice compiled the Android screen and passed 25 unit tests, including preserving list context after returning from a recoverable detail error. The current SwiftUI code also built as a separate arm64 generic iOS Simulator Debug app. Existing APK and universal Debug/Release app evidence are preserved as prior-source snapshots; no new Release rebuild is claimed. Both current Debug artifacts were checked without installation or launch. Missing deployment/accepted live contracts block only real integration, not these independent offline UI fixes. No live requests, new SDKs, permissions or production DTO/endpoints were introduced.

Empty list presentation now requires a completed, error-free, non-partial result for the selected Business/day. A partial response with zero loaded items keeps the partial marker and Retry, without claiming there are no appointments. Controlled unavailable/denied responses and successful empty retry preserve context. The list-state regression was reproduced before the fix on both clients; 26 Android unit tests, 25 SwiftUI host checks and host screen typecheck pass. Earlier app packages predate this last source change; no APK/Xcode/Release build or device/reader acceptance was rerun for it.

Android detail content now scrolls independently below the existing Back/header controls. Long notes are retained; local offline compilation and26 unit checks passed. Device viewport/large-text/TalkBack review remains NOT_RUN; no new APK was packaged.

Appointment detail dates now use the selected Business timezone and the supported UI language instead of displaying raw UTC timestamps. Both native formatters preserve the stored instant and return the existing unavailable label for missing/invalid context. Fixture checks cover RU/KK/EN, selected-zone differences and midnight rollover. Actual Android tests:30 PASS; Swift host checks:25 ViewModel +10 date presentation PASS; actual SwiftUI screen host typecheck PASS. These are source/host checks, not new APK/iOS packages or device/reader acceptance.

The Android date dialog now exposes an explicit dismiss button using the existing localized Back label. Dismiss only closes the dialog: it does not call the ViewModel, change Business/day or issue a reload. Current Android compilation and30 unit checks passed; device/keyboard/reader interaction remains NOT_RUN.

The Android read parser now validates JSON value types explicitly: numeric IDs/totals must be exactly representable by the existing Int model; optional service/resource values must be objects or null. Nullable text/IDs and unknown additional fields remain compatible. Actual regressions reproduced three parser failures before the fix;35 Android unit checks now pass. Swift existing decoding passed87 core checks including16 new malformed/null/forward-field cases. This uses the inspected73482a3b fixture contract, not an accepted CURRENT backend.

## Accepted ecosystem requirement addendum

[ECOSYSTEM_DESKTOP_ADDENDUM_20261029.md](../docs/ECOSYSTEM_DESKTOP_ADDENDUM_20261029.md) is accepted as requirements ECO-01–12. Current delivery is web+iOS+Android; macOS/Windows are a later stage and are not a mobile completion gate. Shared account/data/rights, concurrent sessions, server truth, synchronization/read-unread, notifications/revocation/offline/conflicts are requirements, not implementation PASS. CRM↔Market SSO/access is not implied. CURRENT, auth/provider/device/store and resource gates remain. No desktop development, new framework/dependency or broad refactor starts from this addendum. The immutable U-CANON snapshot remains unchanged.

## Inbox and conversation text (synthetic local flow)

Both Debug clients now have a Messages tab using the existing73482a3b mobile Inbox list and conversation-detail GET projections. Search only examines loaded titles/previews; the unread filter uses the source counter and issues no request. Opening an inquiry loads its own scoped thread; unbooked inquiries remain in Inbox without creating a blank Appointment. Client/Lead/assignee/attachment/href fields are not joined, promoted into rights or used to guess an Appointment association.

The independently loaded thread shows sender, text and Business-local time in server-provided order. Duplicate message IDs, mismatched Business/conversation IDs and invalid message-window counts are rejected. A detail refresh replaces the same header identity, clearing unprojected/withheld old previews. Leaving, retrying or changing Business invalidates old responses/errors, including same-ID reopen. Back preserves search/unread filter/list context; failure and a capped/partial result do not look like complete empty data. Cancelling the current thread returns to the list; cancelling a list leaves retry available. Those cancellation regressions were first reproduced on both platforms and fixed without weakening assertions.

The source returns up to30 recent messages and caps message text at500 characters; the UI does not claim exhaustive history or invent a cursor/load-older route. A full reported window is marked partial. No reply/send/handoff/booking/mark-read action or provider/device transport is added. Legacy unread_count is only displayed: per-user read/unread and multi-client synchronization under ECO-07 remain pending accepted server contracts and integration evidence. Contacts, related history and conversation provenance are not inferred.

Nine exact existing RU/KK/EN labels were reused (37 native keys total). Final local checks:51 Android JUnit tests,110 Swift core checks and71 host ViewModel/presentation checks PASS; all actual SwiftUI App/root/Inbox sources typecheck in Debug and Release host configurations. Project/source membership, shared fixtures/locale consistency and diff checks pass. No new APK/iOS package, app/simulator/device launch, live API, remote CI or store run. Existing packaged apps predate this source packet; visual/reader/device acceptance is NOT_RUN.

## Tasks (synthetic local flow)

Both Debug clients now expose Tasks using the existing73482a3b `build_mobile_tasks` / `_task_item` and `build_mobile_task_detail` GET projections. The list keeps server order, shows source status/priority and Business-local deadlines, and filters only loaded titles/status values. Known status and priority display labels reuse exact existing locale keys; unknown values stay data. Nullable dates/titles remain unavailable rather than being guessed. No overdue/today/terminal-state policy is introduced.

Opening a known task separately reads its description and latest comments. Comment scope comes from the checked Task/Business envelope; the source has no per-comment task_id and the client does not invent one. Duplicate comment IDs, wrong task/Business, invalid deadlines and invalid window sizes are rejected. Related Client/Lead/Deal/Appointment/assignee/author fields are not joined or promoted into rights. The source caps description at1000 and comment text at500 characters and returns up to5 newest comments; preserve that provided order and mark a full reported window partial. No full-history or pagination claim.

Back preserves query/status/list context. Retries and same-ID reopen use distinct request identities; Business change or clearing rejects late responses/errors. Cancellation returns from detail to a recoverable list. The selected status remains a valid picker option while a reload/error/empty result removes its current rows: that regression was reproduced on Android and Swift host before the fix, and assertions remain enabled. The fourth Android tab uses the existing native scrollable tab control. No complete/cancel/assign/snooze/create/edit/comment command is enabled.

Seventeen labels reuse exact RU/KK/EN source keys (54 native keys total). Xcode source membership/plist, shared fixtures/locale parity and diff checks PASS. No new APK/iOS package, app/device/simulator/preview launch, live API, remote CI or store execution. ## Notification center and independent record target (synthetic)

Both Debug clients now expose source-derived notifications with local text/category/unread filtering and a read-only card. Opening the card does not mark read or issue a write. Explicit read_at=null is the legacy source unread marker; a missing marker is unknown and never counted as unread. Per-user and multi-client read state under ECO-07 remains unimplemented pending CURRENT contracts.

A calendar target is offered only for exact supported relative calendar query/path templates matching the notification's positive appointment_id. External/unknown/legacy Lead or Task links, mismatched IDs, extra query/fragment and encoded/traversal variants are not redirected. A target makes its own existing Appointment GET: readable notification does not grant record access. Denial, wrong Business/ID, invalid times, Back/retry and Business switch cannot show an older cached target. Two Back steps preserve the notification card then its filters. The target is an independently read preview inside this flow; it does not alter the global agenda day, create a booking or register an OS deep link.

Source notification text is already capped at220 characters; this card is not a separate full-event detail endpoint. No push, delivery evidence, mark-read, preferences, registration, external URL opening or live API is added. Ten exact source RU/KK/EN labels were reused (64 keys total). Local checks:68 Android JUnit,144 Swift core and103 host ViewModel/presentation checks PASS; all actual SwiftUI App/root code typechecks in Debug/Release host configurations. No new package/app/device/preview/live/store execution. CURRENT notification, recipient/field/branch, target access and ECO sync/provider gates remain separate.

## Independently read client profile and provided visit history (synthetic)

Both Debug clients now open a separate existing Client detail GET from the directory. The profile shows freshly returned contacts and notes, followed by only the Appointment rows explicitly provided in the scoped related projection. It validates Business/Client identity, each related client_id, unique Appointment IDs, time order and the reported window budget. No contact or related entity is reconstructed from a list/cache, matching numeric ID or nearest visit. A failed or denied profile cannot fall back to the old contact card.

Back preserves directory query/list context; Business changes, cancellation, retries and same-ID reopen invalidate older results. An accepted same-scope profile updates its directory contact row and clears redacted nullable fields. A closed selection cannot be refreshed by an old response. Provided history is a read-only preview: the source returns at most5 related rows, a full reported window is marked partial, and an empty provided array is not presented as proof of no history. Lead/Task/Conversation/normalized-contact fields and related rights are not inferred. Create/edit and write commands remain absent.

The shared fixtures use explicit Client/Appointment relations and a matching historical calendar day/detail response; existing agenda records are not relabelled to fabricate a client match. The contract check also verifies these cross-fixture identities and the source's truncated contact-note preview against full profile notes. Existing RU/KK/EN copy is reused without adding labels.

## Home/Today and return to existing sections (synthetic)

Both Debug clients now start with Home/Today. The existing Home GET supplies the Appointment-today, open-task and unread-conversation counts directly; these are not calculated by joining other lists or by treating a service estimate as money. The existing Today GET supplies the dated Appointment and active-due-task previews, totals and window budget. Missing/withheld sections remain unavailable, not zero. The reported total controls partial/confirmed-empty presentation; an empty capped window cannot claim no work. Home and Today have independent loading/error/retry states, so a failed projection does not erase the successful one.

The selected Business is shared with existing native sections. Today sends an explicit date calculated in that Business timezone; the fixed clock is injected only in Debug/tests. The client preserves source date-filter semantics and supplied order, without re-bucketing rows or inventing an overdue policy. Home revenue, Lead/Deal, attention/quick-action URLs and cross-entity fields are not consumed. No new report formula, lifecycle command, server DTO/endpoint or CURRENT contract is introduced.

Four static section buttons open the existing Appointments, Clients, Messages and Tasks sections. Home has no client counter, so the Clients button remains navigation. Summary previews do not guess a detail ID or grant rights; each section independently reads its list and opens the existing scoped detail/profile/thread flow. The explicit Home Back returns to the retained snapshot and preserves each list's query/filter/day. Android system Back first uses the existing detail handler and then returns from the section to Home. Selecting another tab exits the Home return route. A Business change clears the old summary route and invalidates both pending projections; latest-request tickets also reject stale same-scope success/errors and withheld-counter restoration. Cancellation leaves an explicit retry path.

Seventy native UI keys now reuse the existing RU/KK/EN copy map (six labels added in this packet). Shared cross-fixture identities, optional/empty/partial sections, source count types, project membership and whitespace checks pass. These are local source/core/host/unit results, not device/reader acceptance or full app packages. ## Needs attention queue (synthetic read flow)

Both Debug clients now expose the existing mobile Actions projection as a read-only Needs attention queue. It preserves the supplied order and priority values; local search only filters loaded titles. The visible count is the returned window, not the full backlog: build_mobile_actions sets total after sorting and truncation. Every loaded result displays its limit; a full reported window is marked partial. No cursor/load-older route or claim that all work has been processed is invented. Missing, failed, cancelled or denied reads do not become successful empty results.

A read target requires the explicit known key/type pair, positive entity.id and exact corresponding source href template. The address is checked against those fields; no ID is parsed from it and no arbitrary URL is opened. Task, conversation and Appointment targets use the existing independent scoped GET and existing detail projection models. Their Business, target identity, nested-message/comment constraints and time/window validity are checked separately. A readable queue item does not grant target access, and denial has no cached-detail fallback. Source action keys never execute complete, confirm, reply or approval operations.

The task preview includes description/comments, the conversation preview its provided message window, and the Appointment preview its permitted details. They have loading/error/retry/Back states. Back retains queue query/window/order; the accepted target replaces that row's nullable title, including redaction. Request identities reject late success/errors after retry, Back/reopen, page reload or Business change. A cancelled detail returns to the queue with context intact. The queue scroll state is retained separately in Compose; SwiftUI keeps its list beneath the navigation destination. Device gesture/reader/visual acceptance remains NOT_RUN.

Approval and legacy Lead/Deal items, unknown types/keys, absent/non-source/mismatched addresses have no native transition. They retain their source title with an unavailable Open marker; no legacy workflow or approval operation is introduced. Three labels reuse exact existing RU/KK/EN keys (73 total). Shared fixtures explicitly cover the three supported targets, two Businesses and numeric-ID collisions across unsupported/supported entity types. The contract check verifies each supported entity against its separate detail fixture without changing earlier agenda, client, Inbox, task or Home fixtures.

## Daily agenda from actual interval endpoints (synthetic)

Calendar mode now shows a readable daily agenda from the existing Appointment start_at/end_at values, with a compact native day selector. Both endpoints include their full Business-local dates and actual numeric UTC offsets; the selected day and Business timezone remain visible. Overnight intervals are not clipped, and repeated DST wall hours stay distinct by offset. No duration, working hours, empty/free slots, overlap/conflict or resource availability is inferred. List resource information is still unavailable because _appointment_item does not project it. Existing Detail service/resource fields stay separate.

The source list and daily agenda share the same Appointment IDs. List mode retains the returned source order. The daily presentation orders only trustworthy intervals by actual start instant/id and puts uncertain rows in a separate Needs attention section, retaining every permitted identity. Missing or malformed string times, nonpositive intervals and records outside the selected local day remain visible with explicit uncertainty labels. The existing time fields are optional in the read consumer so absent/null values stay unknown; wrong JSON value types remain protocol errors rather than being coerced into dates. This is read robustness, not an accepted CURRENT schema change. The other read-target consumers retain their strict interval guards.

The existing server selector filters start_at__date under its queryset timezone; this is not proof of Business-local bucketing. Request day and response date are kept separately. A wrong response date quarantines its rows without changing the selected day or declaring that day empty, including a zero-row response. A partial result retains its source marker. Partial and mismatched results offer one same-context Retry; no cursor or fabricated total is added. Business/ID/duplicate validation stays strict. A recorded headless check first reproduced the old whole-day rejection caused by one invalid interval; the after checks require that record to remain explicit.

Selection performs a separate scoped detail GET. Fresh same-ID detail fields replace the list row, including redacted/missing times or a record now outside that day; the day is not silently changed. Back retains day, mode, source rows and partial state. SwiftUI navigation now follows the ViewModel detail selection; no destination onDisappear hook can clear a newer request. Compose retains separate list/day scroll states and labels its native day selector. Late list/detail responses, retry/reopen and Business changes remain guarded; cancellation leaves a recoverable read state. No booking/edit/drag/drop/confirm/reschedule/cancel command is added.

The explicit Debug day29 fixture exercises known, overnight, missing, malformed, zero-length and other-day rows with matching independent detail fixtures. Earlier fixture dates are preserved.
